Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The nano operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 458. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 796 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 458 + O(1/n^796). Summing over all modes and applying the nano convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
